==============================================================================
XSESSION
==============================================================================
#!/bin/sh
# XDM session for GNOME on NetBSD.

PATH="/usr/pkg/bin:/usr/pkg/sbin:/usr/X11R7/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"
export PATH

# Recommended by pkgsrc GNOME/mutter on NetBSD.
XCURSOR_PATH="/usr/pkg/share/icons"
export XCURSOR_PATH

# Swedish keyboard layout for X11.
setxkbmap se

# Work around a SpiderMonkey JIT / PaX MPROTECT crash seen in gnome-shell on
# NetBSD 11 with the current mozjs128-based GJS stack.  The failure appears as:
#   MOZ_CRASH([unhandlable oom] ExecutableAllocator::reprotectPool)
# followed by gnome-shell dying with SIGSEGV.
#
# Disabling the GJS JIT avoids executable-page reprotection and keeps NetBSD's
# PaX MPROTECT protection enabled for gnome-shell.
export GJS_DISABLE_JIT=1

# GNOME on NetBSD uses ConsoleKit rather than systemd-logind. Register the
# graphical login session so gnome-session can resolve the current seat/session.
exec ck-launch-session gnome-session

==============================================================================
HOW TO READ THE KTRACE RESULT
==============================================================================
Compare the two GJS ktrace tests.

If the JIT-enabled test crashes but GJS_DISABLE_JIT=1 succeeds, inspect the last
mmap/mprotect call and errno before the signal.

If both succeed, standalone GJS can allocate/reprotect executable memory and the
remaining failure is more specific to gnome-shell or its startup environment.

If both fail, compare the exact errno, PaX flags, and security sysctls before
changing any global security setting.
